Total paths: 0
Format: COMPACT_PATH_V3
All IDs and path indices are unsigned base36 integers.
Dictionary rows:
  N <net_id> <net_name>
  G <gate_id> <output_net_id> <gate_name> <gate_type>
A decoded path token sequence is S = [start_net_id, gate_id_1, ..., gate_id_N].
Token 0 is always the start net; tokens 1..N are always gates.
First record: Path 0: F <every token in S>
Delta record: P <path_index> <prefix_token_count> <suffix_token_count> <middle_tokens>...
IMPORTANT: prefix/suffix counts apply to the entire S, including token 0 (start net).
Decode delta: current S = previous S first <prefix> tokens + middle tokens + previous S last <suffix> tokens.
Example: previous S=[0,0,2,6,7] and 'P 1 2 2 3' decodes to S=[0,0,3,6,7].
Expand names: look up S[0] in net_dictionary; for each gate S[i], look up the gate and then its output_net_id.
Expected paths: 0
